THE BUTTERFLY EFFECT — P0 Butterfly Test
========================================
Rome, AD 155. Your time machine malfunctioned and stranded you here. You have a pouch of gold you scavenged from the
machine (every bit of it must go back before it can fly), what you know, and no one who owes you anything.
You don't yet know what broke. Something is coming from the East within ten years.
Rome runs on silver denarii, not gold: change your aurei at the money changers (exchange <n> aurei). Gold holds its value; the denarius doesn't.
Once you repair it, the machine can carry you forward, 25 to 60 years depending on how well you repair it. What you leave behind will go on without you.

This prototype covers one era, a jump and your arrival, and then, if you like, one more jump to see how it all aged. The test ends there.

First, a choice. You can afford only one:
  choose fountain  — The Subura's fountain runs brown. Clear the lead pipe and reline the basin.
  choose workshop  — Pay for a second forge and paid apprentices at the smith's by the Porta Trigemina, in exchange for a share of the business.
Your ears still ring from the crash. Before anything else you could walk and get your bearings: visit market, changers, forges, curia or subura (no Attention).
Type 'help' for commands.

== Month 1 · Ianuarius, AD 155 ==  0 denarii (+0/month) · 60 aurei (1 = 25 den.)   Index 100
  Attention: 4 free / 4 total
  Medicine      50  expect   50  (no voice)  debt     0 Stable
  Governance    55  expect   55  (no voice)  debt     0 Stable
  Economy       55  expect   55  (no voice)  debt     0 Stable
  News: Antoninus Pius has ruled for seventeen years and hasn't left Italy in all that time. People call it the quietest reign anyone can remember.   (news)
  Machine: not yet assessed, gold 0/60 aurei   (machine)
  ► Waiting: choose fountain or choose workshop (before the end of turn 8).

> choose fountain
You chose the fountain.  [Attention left this month: 2/4]

> assess
Started: Assess the machine.  [Attention left this month: 1/4]

> repair coil
You don't know yet what's wrong with the machine. Assess it first (assess: 1 Attention a month for 2 months).

> repair coolant
You don't know yet what's wrong with the machine. Assess it first (assess: 1 Attention a month for 2 months).

> repair chronometer
You don't know yet what's wrong with the machine. Assess it first (assess: 1 Attention a month for 2 months).

> promise yes
No promise is waiting for an answer.

> respond hospice
There is no outbreak to respond to.

> respond quarantine
There is no outbreak to respond to.

> found school
Founding the School of the Fountain costs 3,850 denarii.

> charter school
Found it first (found school).

> oversee school
Found it first (found school).

> start physician
Fund a physician for the district costs 2,250 denarii; you have 0 denarii.

> start market
Honest weights and free prices at the Forum Boarium is public business: you need 10% of an institution in Economy to push it through.

> start census
Sponsor a household census is public business: you need 10% of an institution in Governance to push it through.

> start magistrates
Fund the district magistrates is public business: you need 10% of an institution in Governance to push it through.

> work
You earn 125 denarii; tax takes 12 denarii, you keep 112 denarii.  [Attention left this month: 0/4]
  (No Attention left this month. Type 'end' to end the month.)

> end

== Month 243 · Martius, AD 175 ==  17,189 denarii (+13/month) · 34 aurei (1 = 33.7 den.)   Index 126
  Attention: 3 free / 4 total · 1 reserved — Duties at the School
  Medicine    81.3  expect 81.3  Maintain    debt     0 Stable
  Governance    53  expect   53  (no voice)  debt     0 Stable
  Economy     52.6  expect 52.6  (no voice)  debt     0 Stable
  Prices: +34.7% since AD 155 (1.5% a year)
  Pestilence: The pestilence has passed.
  The School (Theon of Alexandria): you hold 100% (control), strength 97 (54.8% of Medicine), loyalty 90, chartered
  Machine: 9/9 repair steps, gold 60/60 aurei — ready to jump   (machine)
  ► The era's 20 years are over. Jump when you're ready (you can also stay).

> jump
You will leave AD 175 and arrive somewhere between AD 210 and AD 225: the machine's range is 35–50 years (repairs, 0 of 3 upgrades, and your time in this era). You can't come back.
What you leave behind (17,189 denarii and 34 aurei in hand):
  • No last orders for the School (leading now: the Academy of the Fountain): orders school academy|secret [1|2 to name who succeeds you: 1 Theon of Alexandria, your first teacher at the school, careful and slow; 2 Hermogenes the Younger, brilliant and ambitious, and eager to take the school into the great houses].
  • The School of the Fountain would be left bare (chartered but not endowed, so it counts as bare), losing 10% of its strength each decade (now 97).
  •   It holds no money, so it can't pay down Medicine debt while you're away. (endow school <denarii>)
  • The machine will carry you 35–50 years (repairs, 0 of 3 upgrades, and your time in this era); exactly how far, you'll know when you arrive. Upgrades and more time here would lengthen it.
  • The 17,189 denarii in your hands stay behind and are lost unless you spend them, pay down debt or endow an institution.
  • Your gold: the machine can carry 10 aurei; you hold 34 aurei, so 24 aurei would be left behind and lost (deposit <n> or bury <n>, 1 Attention each; the bank takes up to 100 aurei, a jar holds 50 aurei).
  •   Denarii can't be carried, banked or buried usefully: change them into gold first (exchange <n> denarii).
Prepare: paydown <domain> <points> · endow <inst> <denarii|all> · audit <inst> · exchange <n> denarii · deposit <aurei> · bury <aurei>. Type 'jump' again to go, or anything else to stay.

> paydown medicine 9999
Medicine has no debt to pay down.

> endow school all
The School holds 17,175 denarii.  [Attention left this month: 1/4]
What you leave behind (14 denarii and 34 aurei in hand):
  • No last orders for the School (leading now: the Academy of the Fountain): orders school academy|secret [1|2 to name who succeeds you: 1 Theon of Alexandria, your first teacher at the school, careful and slow; 2 Hermogenes the Younger, brilliant and ambitious, and eager to take the school into the great houses].
  • The School of the Fountain would be left strong, losing 1% of its strength each decade (now 97).
  •   It holds 17,175 denarii (large), growing about 0.8% a year with the economy for 30 years.
  •   It would pay in full toward Medicine debt (now 0); its money covers about 229 points at the 1.5× premium.
  •   Corruption risk: Medium for 30 years (large holdings, no audit charter, Theon of Alexandria is honest). If it happens, it could be minor (50%), major (40%) or total (10%). (audit school: 1,000 denarii)
  • The machine will carry you 35–50 years (repairs, 0 of 3 upgrades, and your time in this era); exactly how far, you'll know when you arrive. Upgrades and more time here would lengthen it.
  • Your gold: the machine can carry 10 aurei; you hold 34 aurei, so 24 aurei would be left behind and lost (deposit <n> or bury <n>, 1 Attention each; the bank takes up to 100 aurei, a jar holds 50 aurei).
Prepare: paydown <domain> <points> · endow <inst> <denarii|all> · audit <inst> · exchange <n> denarii · deposit <aurei> · bury <aurei>. Type 'jump' again to go, or anything else to stay.

> jump

The machine shudders. Decades pass in the dark... It carries you 35 years.

— Recognition —
You climb out of the machine in AD 210. The Tiber smells the same. In the Subura, the district
fountain you repaired still runs: the basin is worn smooth, and someone has cut two words into its
rim: AQUA PURA. Of the smith you didn't choose, there is no trace.

— Wrongness —
Something is wrong, and it takes you a moment to see it: children, everywhere. More than any Roman
street you remember, and fewer small graves along the Via Appia. The coins are lighter and paler than
you remember, barely half silver now. Prices have climbed with every cut, and soldiers grumble that
their pay buys less each year.

— Personal echo —
In an old house in the Subura you find a line carved over a door, in Demetria's Greek: 'For the one
who stayed.' They don't know it means you. Over the door of the School of the Fountain someone has
painted its beginning in red letters: 'Begun by a stranger from the East.'

— Discovery —
The School of the Fountain, which you founded, is still there and still itself. Its head is a
successor of Theon of Alexandria. In your purse: the 10 aurei you carried, as good as the day you
left. The gold you couldn't carry, and didn't hide or bank (24 aurei), is long gone.

You can walk around Rome: visit market, changers, forges, curia or subura (no Attention).
Type 'learn more' for the Index and what became of your institutions, 'jump' to go on another 25–40 years, or 'quit'.

Crises while you were away
  AD 189: the pestilence returns to Rome under Commodus. At its worst two thousand die in a day; the emperor goes to Laurentum for the clean air. It is contained: about 6 thousand dead, 1% of Rome.


(after arrival) > why medicine
You have been away for 35 years. What happened in between is lost to you; the world simply is what it is now. (Try 'learn more'.)

(after arrival) > quit
